class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ILGA_2013_1-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ILGA 2013","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-ILGA_2013-1" id="mwCQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwCg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwCw">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwDA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt7" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_Maan_2021_2-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA Maan 2021","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_Maan_2021-2" id="mwDQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwDg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwDw">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwEA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt9" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_2022_Pride_Response_3-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA 2022 Pride Response","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_2022_Pride_Response-3" id="mwEQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwEg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwEw">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwFA">]</span></span></a></sup></td></tr><tr><th scope="row" class="infobox-label"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LGBT_people_and_military_service" title="LGBT people and military service" class="mw-redirect">Military</a></th><td class="infobox-data">No</td></tr><tr><th scope="row" class="infobox-label"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-discrimination_law" title="Anti-discrimination law">Discrimination protections</a></th><td class="infobox-data">No</td></tr><tr><th colspan="2" class="infobox-header">Family rights</th></tr><tr><th scope="row" class="infobox-label"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Template:Same-sex_unions" title="Template:Same-sex unions">Recognition of relationships</a></th><td class="infobox-data">No</td></tr><tr><th scope="row" class="infobox-label"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Same-sex_adoption" title="Same-sex adoption">Adoption</a></th><td class="infobox-data">No</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p id="mwFQ">Lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender and queer (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LGBTQ_community" title="LGBTQ community" id="mwFg">LGBTQ</a>) people living in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bahrain" title="Bahrain" id="mwFw">Bahrain</a> face significant challenges and discrimination not experienced by non-LGBTQ residents.<sup about="#mwt11" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ILGA_2013_1-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ILGA 2013","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ILGA 2013"},"2":{"wt":"albilad 2022"},"3":{"wt":"BNA Maan 2021"},"4":{"wt":"BNA 2022 Pride Response"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-ILGA_2013-1" id="mwGA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwGQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGg">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwGw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt11" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwHA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwHQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwHg">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwHw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt11" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_Maan_2021_2-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA Maan 2021","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_Maan_2021-2" id="mwIA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwIQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIg">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwIw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt11" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_2022_Pride_Response_3-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA 2022 Pride Response","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_2022_Pride_Response-3" id="mwJA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwJQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJg">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwJw">]</span></span></a></sup> While same-sex sexual activity was decriminalised in 1976, laws against indecency remain and are used to target gender and sexual minorities. Offences under these provisions allow for sentences of imprisonment, fines and deportation.<sup about="#mwt20" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ussd_21_5-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ussd 21","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ussd 21"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-ussd_21-5" id="mwKA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwKQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKg">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwKw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwLA">Individuals are able to change their legal gender in a limited range of circumstances that are assessed as being in accordance with Islamic understandings of sex and gender as transmitted by Sunni <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt23" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwLQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"[[fiqh]]"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fiqh" title="Fiqh">fiqh</a></i></span>. The state offers no protection from discrimination on the basis of sexual orientation, gender identity or expression, or sex characteristics. LGBTQ individuals face entrenched social stigma.<sup about="#mwt24" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_ussd_6-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 ussd"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_ussd-6" id="mwLg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwLw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwMA">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwMQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt25" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-cairo52_7-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"cairo52"}}'><a href="#cite_note-cairo52-7" id="mwMg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwMw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNA">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwNQ">]</span></span></a></sup> Discussion of homosexuality has been permitted in Bahrain since the 1990s.</p>

<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" id="mwNg" data-mw='{"autoGenerated":true}'/></section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwNw" aria-labelledby="Laws"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Laws">Laws</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Laws"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt26" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwOA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"See also","href":"./Template:See_also"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Homosexuality"},"2":{"wt":"Gay men"},"3":{"wt":"Lesbian women"},"4":{"wt":"Bisexuality"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1368532237" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt27" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'>.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+.mw-empty-elt+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}@media print{body.ns-0 .mw-parser-output .hatnote{display:none!important}}</style></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt26" id="mwOQ">See also: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Homosexuality" title="Homosexuality">Homosexuality</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gay_men" title="Gay men">Gay men</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lesbian_women" title="Lesbian women" class="mw-redirect">Lesbian women</a>, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bisexuality" title="Bisexuality">Bisexuality</a></div>

<section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwOg" aria-labelledby="History"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="History">History</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: History"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt28" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwOw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"See also","href":"./Template:See_also"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"History of LGBT"},"2":{"wt":"LGBTQ people and Islam"},"3":{"wt":""},"4":{"wt":""},"5":{"wt":""},"6":{"wt":""}},"i":0}}]}'><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt29" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'/></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt28" id="mwPA">See also: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/History_of_LGBT" title="History of LGBT" class="mw-redirect">History of LGBT</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LGBTQ_people_and_Islam" title="LGBTQ people and Islam">LGBTQ people and Islam</a></div>
<p id="mwPQ">Homosexual acts were first criminalised in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bahrain" title="Bahrain" id="mwPg">Bahrain</a> as "<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sodomy" title="Sodomy" id="mwPw">sodomy</a>" while it was a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/History_of_Bahrain_(1783–1971)#British_Protectorate" title="History of Bahrain (1783–1971)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwQA">protectorate of the British</a>.<sup about="#mwt30" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ILGA_2013_1-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ILGA 2013"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ILGA_2013-1" id="mwQQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwQg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwQw">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRA">]</span></span></a></sup> Similar laws were imposed throughout the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/British_Empire" title="British Empire" id="mwRQ">British Empire</a> in nearly all of its colonies; these were mostly modelled on the original 1860 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Section_377" title="Section 377" id="mwRg">Section 377</a>, introduced in India by the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/British_Raj" title="British Raj" id="mwRw">Raj</a>.<sup about="#mwt33" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-8" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-8" id="mwSA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwSQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSg">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt36" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-9" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-9" id="mwTA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwTQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTg">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt39" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-10" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-10" id="mwUA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwUQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUg">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwVA" aria-labelledby="Legality_of_same-sex_sexual_acts"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Legality_of_same-sex_sexual_acts">Legality of same-sex sexual acts</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Legality of same-sex sexual acts"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwVQ">A new Penal Code was enacted in March 1976, repealing the Penal Code of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Persian_Gulf" title="Persian Gulf" id="mwVg">Persian Gulf</a> that was imposed by the British.<sup about="#mwt43" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ILGA_2013_1-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ILGA 2013"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ILGA_2013-1" id="mwVw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwWA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWQ">[</span>1<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWg">]</span></span></a></sup> The new penal code does not prohibit private, non-commercial acts of homosexuality between consenting adults; for the purposes of this law, "adults" are at least 21 years old.<sup about="#mwt40" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ilga_2020_11-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ilga 2020","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ilga 2020"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-ilga_2020-11" id="mwWw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwXA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXQ">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwXw">Although no law explicitly criminalizes <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Same-sex_relationship" title="Same-sex relationship" id="mwYA">same-sex relations</a>, authorities have used broadly-phrased penal code provisions against "indecency" and "immorality" or "debauchery" (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt44" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwYQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"فجور"},"3":{"wt":"fujoor"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt44">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt44">فجور</span><span about="#mwt44">, </span><small about="#mwt44"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romanization_of_Arabic" title="Romanization of Arabic">romanized</a>:<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></small><span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt44"><i lang="ar-Latn">fujoor</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt44" id="mwYg"/>) to target these minorities.<sup about="#mwt45" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-state_2017_12-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"state 2017","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"state 2017"},"2":{"wt":"2023 ussd"},"3":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-state_2017-12" id="mwYw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwZA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZQ">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwZg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt45" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_ussd_6-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 ussd","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_ussd-6" id="mwZw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwaA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaQ">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwag">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt45" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwaw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwbA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbQ">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwbw">The 2023 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_Department_of_State" title="United States Department of State" id="mwcA">United States Department of State</a>'s report said:<sup about="#mwt52" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_ussd_6-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 ussd"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_ussd-6" id="mwcQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwcg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcw">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwdA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1244412712" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" about="#mwt53" id="mwdQ" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"text":{"wt":"The law did not criminalize same-sex sexual conduct between consenting adults. It prescribed fines, imprisonment, deportation, or a combination thereof for persons engaging in \"debauchery\" or \"public indecency\". The government used this provision against individuals suspected of being lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender, queer, or intersex (LGBTQI+) or of\" cross-dressing\"."},"title":{"wt":"Bahrain - United States Department of State"},"source":{"wt":"Acts of Violence, Criminalization, and Other Abuses Based on Sexual Orientation, Gender Identity or Expression, or Sex Characteristics"}},"i":0}}]}'>.mw-parser-output .templatequote{overflow:hidden;margin:1em 0;padding:0 32px}.mw-parser-output .templatequotecite{line-height:1.5em;text-align:left;margin-top:0}@media(min-width:500px){.mw-parser-output .templatequotecite{padding-left:1.6em}}</style><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt53"><p>The law did not criminalize same-sex sexual conduct between consenting adults. It prescribed fines, imprisonment, deportation, or a combination thereof for persons engaging in "debauchery" or "public indecency". The government used this provision against individuals suspected of being lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender, queer, or intersex (LGBTQI+) or of" cross-dressing".</p></blockquote><div class="templatequotecite" about="#mwt53" id="mwdg"><p style="display: inline; padding-left: 2.3em;">—<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span>Bahrain - United States Department of State, Acts of Violence, Criminalization, and Other Abuses Based on Sexual Orientation, Gender Identity or Expression, or Sex Characteristics<span style="display:none" data-plural="1"></span></p></div>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="4" id="mwdw" aria-labelledby="Interpretation"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Interpretation">Interpretation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Interpretation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mweA">According to an article published by Al-Bilad in 2022, which discussed a recent debate in Bahrain about homosexuality, sparked by an incident where a private school mistakenly distributed material perceived as promoting it.<sup about="#mwt55" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mweQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mweg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwew">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwfA">]</span></span></a></sup> This led to calls for stricter laws and investigations.<sup about="#mwt58" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwfQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwfg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwfw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwgA">]</span></span></a></sup> The Bahraini law already criminalizes "debauchery" and prostitution, with penalties for incitement or promotion of such acts.<sup about="#mwt61" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwgQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwgg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwgw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhA">]</span></span></a></sup> Article 324 of the Bahraini Penal Code criminalizes inciting or assisting "debauchery" (fujur) or prostitution, which are defined as habitual immoral acts.<sup about="#mwt64" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwhQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwhg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwiA">]</span></span></a></sup> This law applies to individuals who promote or facilitate such acts (a woman inviting others to commit.<sup about="#mwt67" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwiQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwig"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwiw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjA">]</span></span></a></sup> Homosexuality is implicitly associated with "debauchery", particularly in the case of men engaging in same-sex relations. <sup about="#mwt70" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwjQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwjg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwkA">]</span></span></a></sup> However, the law does not explicitly define or address homosexuality as a distinct crime.<sup about="#mwt73" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-8" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwkQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwkg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwkw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlA">]</span></span></a></sup> Lawmakers argue that homosexuality isn't explicitly addressed, creating a "legislative gap."<sup about="#mwt76" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-9" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwlQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwlg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwlw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwmA">]</span></span></a></sup> Lawyer Mahmood Rabe'a has pointed out that Article 324 of the Penal Code penalizes anyone who incites a man or a woman to commit debauchery or prostitution, or assists in this by any means, with imprisonment.<sup about="#mwt79" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-10" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwmQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwmg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwmw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwnA">]</span></span></a></sup> He clarified that the interpretation of this article, according to jurisprudence and judiciary, stipulates that the crime of debauchery or prostitution is only realized through engaging in indecent acts with people indiscriminately and habitually, whether involving male or female prostitution.<sup about="#mwt82" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-11" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwnQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwng"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwnw">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwoA">]</span></span></a></sup> He added that when a woman commits indecent acts (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt85" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwoQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"الفحشاء"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt85">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt85">الفحشاء</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt85" id="mwog"/>) and offers herself to anyone without distinction, this constitutes prostitution (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt86" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwow" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"بغي"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt86">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt86">بغي</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt86" id="mwpA"/>), which is attributed to women and does not come from men. Its counterpart is debauchery (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt87" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwpQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"فجور"},"3":{"wt":"fujoor"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt87">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt87">فجور</span><span about="#mwt87">, </span><small about="#mwt87"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Romanization_of_Arabic" title="Romanization of Arabic">romanized</a>:<span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></small><span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt87"><i lang="ar-Latn">fujoor</i></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt87" id="mwpg"/>), which is attributed to men when they offer themselves to other men indiscriminately and habitually.<sup about="#mwt88" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-12" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwpw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwqA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwqQ">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwqg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwqw">Attempts have been made to enact harsher anti-LGBTQ laws; <span about="#mwt91" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwrA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"as of","href":"./Template:As_of"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"December 2024"},"lc":{"wt":"y"}},"i":0}}]}'>as of December 2024</span><sup class="plainlinks noexcerpt noprint asof-tag update" style="display:none;" about="#mwt91"><a rel="mw:ExtLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit" class="external text" data-mw-original-href="//en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span>update<span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></a></sup><span typeof="mw:Nowiki" about="#mwt91"></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_potentially_dated_statements_from_December_2024" about="#mwt91"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_containing_potentially_dated_statements" about="#mwt91" id="mwrQ"/>, none have gone through. A bill proposed by some members of parliament to amend the penal code in October 2021, included renaming one of its chapters "Debauchery, Prostitution, and Perversion",<sup about="#mwt92" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-13" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwrg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwrw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsA">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsQ">]</span></span></a></sup> and the addition of two articles criminalizing "[raising] a flag, slogan, or any sign symbolizing homosexuals", "[promoting] the ideas and beliefs of homosexuals" and "[inviting, organizing or attending] any gathering or meeting of homosexuals", punishable by up to 5 years' imprisonment and a fine of 3,000-5,000 dinars.<sup about="#mwt95" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-14" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwsg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwsw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwtA">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwtQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt98" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-long_13-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"long","group":"lower-alpha"},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"efn","href":"./Template:Efn"},"params":{"name":{"wt":"\"long\""}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-long-13" data-mw-group="lower-alpha" id="mwtg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwtw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwuA">[</span>a<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwuQ">]</span></span></a></sup> The term used in the bill to refer to homosexuals is the derogatory <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt101" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwug" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"Al-shawadh Jinsian"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">Al-shawadh Jinsian</i></span> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt102" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwuw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"الشواذ جنسيا"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt102">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt102">الشواذ جنسيا</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt102" id="mwvA"/>).<sup about="#mwt105" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Almaany_shududh_14-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Almaany shududh"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Almaany_shududh-14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Almaany_shududh-14" id="mwvQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwvg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwvw">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwwA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt108" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Palmer_2012_15-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Palmer 2012"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Palmer_2012-15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Palmer_2012-15" id="mwwQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwwg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwww">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt111" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Clemons_2012_16-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Clemons 2012"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Clemons_2012-16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Clemons_2012-16" id="mwxQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwxg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwxw">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwyA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="5" id="mwyQ" aria-labelledby="Relationship_law"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Relationship_law">Relationship law</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=5" title="Edit section: Relationship law"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwyg">For Bahraini Muslim citizens, marriage is defined and otherwise regulated by the National Personal Status Law (2017), which does not recognize same-sex marriages.<sup about="#mwt114" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-17" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-17"}}'><a href="#cite_note-17" id="mwyw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwzA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwzQ">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwzg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwzw">According to an interview conducted in 2023 by Ibraheem Al-Nahham for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Bilad_(Bahraini_newspaper)" title="Al Bilad (Bahraini newspaper)" id="mw0A">Al-Bilad Newspaper</a>; a pro-government newspaper, Bahraini lawyer Fareed Ghazi (member of "liberal party" <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Economists_Bloc" title="Economists Bloc" id="mw0Q">Economists Bloc</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Muntada" title="Al Muntada" id="mw0g">Al Muntada</a> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liberalism" title="Liberalism" id="mw0w">liberal</a> think tank) stated that Bahraini law criminalizes same-sex relationships outside the framework of traditional Islamic marriage.<sup about="#mwt116" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:3_18-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":3"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:3-18" id="mw1A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw1Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1g">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1w">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt119" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:4_19-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":4"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:4-19"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:4-19" id="mw2A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw2Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw2g">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw2w">]</span></span></a></sup> Ghazi cited religious beliefs and cultural practices as the basis for these legal restrictions, arguing that they are consistent with the country's cultural norms, further stating that any talk about homosexuality in the Kingdom of Bahrain is a violation of Islamic law and the customs and traditions of Bahraini society (referring to the US embassy which celebrates <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pride_Month" title="Pride Month" id="mw3A">pride month</a> each year).<sup about="#mwt122" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:3_18-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":3"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:3-18"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:3-18" id="mw3Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw3g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw3w">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw4A">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt123" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:4_19-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:4-19" id="mw4Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw4g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw4w">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw5A">]</span></span></a></sup> The interview did not address the existence of homosexual individuals in Bahrain or the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Biology_and_sexual_orientation" title="Biology and sexual orientation" id="mw5Q">scientific perspectives</a> on homosexuality. The LGBTQ community in Bahrain is often spoken about from a negative religious "Islamic" perspective.<sup about="#mwt126" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_20-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:7-20"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-20" id="mw5g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw5w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw6A">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw6Q">]</span></span></a></sup> This reflects a broader pattern in some Islamic societies, where new scientific and technological developments, such as the internet, have faced resistance or controversy.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_minor_POV_problems" about="#mwt115" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mw6g" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"opinion","href":"./Template:Opinion"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"January 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_minor_POV_problems_from_January_2025" about="#mwt115"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template noprint Template-Opinion" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt115" id="mw6w"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Neutral_point_of_view/FAQ#Assert_facts,_not_opinions" title="Wikipedia:Neutral point of view/FAQ"><span title="This statement may be opinion presented as fact. (January 2025)">opinion</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup><sup about="#mwt129" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-21" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-21"}}'><a href="#cite_note-21" id="mw7A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw7Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw7g">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw7w">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="6" id="mw8A" aria-labelledby="Related_penal_code_provisions"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Related_penal_code_provisions">Related penal code provisions</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=6" title="Edit section: Related penal code provisions"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul id="mw8Q"><li id="mw8g">Article 324 of the penal code prohibits enticing another person to commit prostitution or "<span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt130" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mw8w" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"fojoor"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">fojoor</i></span>" or "<span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt131" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mw9A" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"fujoor"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">fujoor</i></span>". This translates to 'immorality' and seems to have vagueness in terms of its legal definition. It has increasingly been used to crack down on men who wear women's clothing in public.<sup about="#mwt135" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-22" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-22"}}'><a href="#cite_note-22" id="mw9Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw9g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw9w">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw-A">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mw-Q">Articles 325–327 involve forcing of others to become prostitutes.</li>
<li id="mw-g">Article 328 of the penal code prohibits running a place for prostitution or <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt136" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mw-w" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"fojoor"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">fojoor</i></span>.<sup about="#mwt139" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-crim_code_23-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"crim code"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-crim_code-23"}}'><a href="#cite_note-crim_code-23" id="mw_A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw_Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw_g">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw_w">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAQA">Article 329 of the penal code prohibits people from public solicitation involving prostitution or <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt140" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAQE" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"fujoor"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">fujoor</i></span>.<sup about="#mwt141" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-crim_code_23-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"crim code"}}'><a href="#cite_note-crim_code-23" id="mwAQI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAQM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQQ">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQU">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAQY">Article 330 of the penal code states that anyone who is charged with an act of prostitution or <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt142" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAQc" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"fujoor"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">fujoor</i></span> is to be taken to a hospital and tested for sexually transmitted diseases. If they have such diseases, the law stipulates that they be relocated to a medical facility for treatment.<sup about="#mwt143" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-crim_code_23-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"crim code"}}'><a href="#cite_note-crim_code-23" id="mwAQg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAQk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQo">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQs">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAQw">Article No. 350 of the Penal Code of Decree Law No. 15 of 1976 prohibits any sort of public indecency. The punishment for those “imitating the opposite sex” can reach up to a year in prison with 100 BHD fines.<sup about="#mwt146" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-24" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-24"}}'><a href="#cite_note-24" id="mwAQ0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAQ4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAQ8">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt149" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-25" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-25"}}'><a href="#cite_note-25" id="mwARE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARM">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARQ">]</span></span></a></sup> This could pose serious risks for any individual who does not fit with societal norms and expectations (appearance wise).<sup about="#mwt150" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-crim_code_23-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"crim code"}}'><a href="#cite_note-crim_code-23" id="mwARU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARc">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARg">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwARk">Article 354 of the penal code prohibits cursing or using words or signs on a street or other public place for the purposes of indulging in immoral behaviour.<sup about="#mwt151" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-crim_code_23-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"crim code"}}'><a href="#cite_note-crim_code-23" id="mwARo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwARs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwARw">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAR0">]</span></span></a></sup></li></ul>

<p id="mwAR4">Penalties for convictions under the morality or indecency provisions are prison sentences ranging from 3 months to 2 years, fines of up to <span about="#mwt152" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAR8" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"currency","href":"./Template:Currency"},"params":{"amount":{"wt":"200"},"code":{"wt":"BHD"},"first":{"wt":"y"}},"i":0}}]}'>200 </span><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bahraini_dinar" title="Bahraini dinar" about="#mwt152" id="mwASA">Bahraini dinars</a>, deportation for non-Bahrainis, or a combination.<sup about="#mwt153" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_ussd_6-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 ussd"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_ussd-6" id="mwASE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwASI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASM">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwASU">Articles 324, 329 and 350 can be used against homosexuals, transgender people, or any other Queer individuals, as any citizen acting in a way deemed contrary to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morality_in_Islam" title="Morality in Islam" id="mwASY">Islamic morality</a> may be arrested. For example: a local same-sex couple showing affection in public; someone owning a rainbow flag; or even declaring one's own homosexuality, could all be considered public immorality and promoting "<span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt154" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwASc" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"fujoor"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">fujoor</i></span>".<sup about="#mwt157" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Freedom_of_Thought_Report_2019_26-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Freedom of Thought Report 2019"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Freedom_of_Thought_Report_2019-26"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Freedom_of_Thought_Report_2019-26" id="mwASg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwASk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASo">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwASs">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt160" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-27" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-27"}}'><a href="#cite_note-27" id="mwASw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAS0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAS4">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAS8">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwATA" aria-labelledby="Gender_identity_and_expression"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Gender_identity_and_expression">Gender identity and expression</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=7" title="Edit section: Gender identity and expression"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt161" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwATE" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"See also","href":"./Template:See_also"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Gender identity"},"2":{"wt":"Gender-affirming surgery"}},"i":0}}]}'><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt162" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'/></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt161" id="mwATI">See also: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gender_identity" title="Gender identity">Gender identity</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gender-affirming_surgery" title="Gender-affirming surgery">Gender-affirming surgery</a></div>

<section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwATM" aria-labelledby="Gender_change_recognition"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Gender_change_recognition">Gender change recognition</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=8" title="Edit section: Gender change recognition"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwATQ">In 2017, the United States Department of State described processes for gender change recognition in Bahrain this way:<sup about="#mwt163" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-state_2017_12-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"state 2017"}}'><a href="#cite_note-state_2017-12" id="mwATU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwATY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATc">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwATg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1244412712" about="#mwt164" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwATk" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"On rare occasions, courts approved the issuance of new legal documents for those who have undergone gender reassignment surgeries. On 23 November, the courts denied a citizen who underwent gender reassignment surgery the right to change his name and identity documents to match his sex; the case was still pending final appeal before the Court of Cassation as at year&apos;s end."}},"i":0}}]}'/><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt164" id="mwATo"><p>On rare occasions, courts approved the issuance of new legal documents for those who have undergone gender reassignment surgeries. On 23 November, the courts denied a citizen who underwent gender reassignment surgery the right to change his name and identity documents to match his sex; the case was still pending final appeal before the Court of Cassation as at year's end.</p></blockquote><p id="mwATs"> The State Department's 2023 report said:<sup about="#mwt166" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_ussd_6-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 ussd"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_ussd-6" id="mwATw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAT0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAT4">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAT8">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1244412712" about="#mwt167" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwAUA" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Courts permitted individuals to change their gender identity markers on legal and identifying national documents to bring them into alignment with their gender identity (as male or female). Individuals had to provide medical documentation of a \"sex change operation\" or medical necessity. Nonbinary, intersex, or gender nonconforming options were not available."}},"i":0}}]}'/><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt167" id="mwAUE"><p>Courts permitted individuals to change their gender identity markers on legal and identifying national documents to bring them into alignment with their gender identity (as male or female). Individuals had to provide medical documentation of a "sex change operation" or medical necessity. Nonbinary, intersex, or gender nonconforming options were not available.</p></blockquote>
<p id="mwAUI">Two such "rare cases" in the 2000s were those of individuals designated female at birth who identified as male. One,
having undergone a genital reconstruction surgery, was reported by the <i id="mwAUM"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulf_Daily_News" title="Gulf Daily News" id="mwAUQ">Gulf Daily News</a></i> in 2006 as going to court in a bid to have his status as a man recognised in law. The legal case progressed through the Bahraini legal system until 2008, when the court granted the motion to allow the transgender man to change his legal documents and be recognised as male. The man's lawyer had earlier won a landmark 2005 case where a transgender Bahraini had undergone sex-confirming surgery and was legally recognised as a man. In both cases, the individuals concerned, although raised as females, were intersex or had some degree of genital or reproductive organ ambiguity.<sup about="#mwt171" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:8_28-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":8"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:8-28"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:8-28" id="mwAUU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAUY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUc">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUg">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt174" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:9_29-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":9"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:9-29"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:9-29" id="mwAUk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAUo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUs">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAUw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAU0">In 2023, Bahraini courts gave a ruling, in rejecting a transgender man's application, that access to sex reassignment medical procedures was against Islamic law in cases considered to be solely related to <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gender_dysphoria" title="Gender dysphoria" id="mwAU4">gender dysphoria</a>; only individuals with some degree of intersex biological traits should be eligible for gender-confirming procedures, and thereafter legal recognition of gender change.<sup about="#mwt177" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-cairo52_7-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"cairo52"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-cairo52-7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-cairo52-7" id="mwAU8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVE">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVI">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwAVM" aria-labelledby="Government_policies_and_actions"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Government_policies_and_actions">Government policies and actions</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=9" title="Edit section: Government policies and actions"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwAVQ">The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Menbar_Islamic_Society" title="Al-Menbar Islamic Society" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAVU">Al-Menbar Islamic Society</a> is one of the more successful political factions within the Parliament, it has pushed for more conservative social policies, including a crackdown on LGBTQ people.<sup about="#mwt180" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Alwasat_AlMenbar_2004_30-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Alwasat AlMenbar 2004"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Alwasat_AlMenbar_2004-30"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Alwasat_AlMenbar_2004-30" id="mwAVY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVg">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVk">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt183" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Al-Menbar_2022_31-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Al-Menbar 2022"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Al-Menbar_2022-31"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Al-Menbar_2022-31" id="mwAVo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAVs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAVw">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAV0">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAV4">In response to questions from parliament about lesbianism in schools, the Assistant Under-Secretary for Educational Services Khalid Al Alawi has said that the Education Ministry is not responsible for addressing issues of sexuality, and instead it is the responsibility of parents to take care of their children's emotional development, and that "The public shouldn't make a big deal out of this problem because it does not exist."  Also, he said "<span class="nowrap" about="#mwt184" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAV8" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"nbsp","href":"./Template:Nbsp"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}'><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span>... the students are facing<span class="nowrap" about="#mwt185" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAWA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"nbsp","href":"./Template:Nbsp"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}'><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span>... [the] category of educational problems, not immoral acts. If a student's appearance is contrary to custom and the school's values, then <span class="nowrap" about="#mwt186" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAWE" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"nbsp","href":"./Template:Nbsp"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}'><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></span>... those violating the school's rules should be disciplined."<sup about="#mwt190" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-32" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-32"}}'><a href="#cite_note-32" id="mwAWI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAWM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWQ">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAWY">In 2008, a harsher crackdown on same-sex sexual acts was called for by members of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Menbar_Progressive_Democratic_Society" title="Al-Menbar Progressive Democratic Society" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAWc">Al Menbar</a> parliamentary bloc. The government was asked to conduct an official study into the problem of same-sex sexual acts and how to best combat them. The initial response from the government was:</p>
<ul id="mwAWg"><li id="mwAWk">The Interior Minister says that "suspected" (effeminate) homosexuals are banned from entering Bahrain by checks at the airport.<sup about="#mwt193" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-gulf-daily-news1_33-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"gulf-daily-news1"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-gulf-daily-news1-33"}}'><a href="#cite_note-gulf-daily-news1-33" id="mwAWo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAWs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAWw">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAW0">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAW4">The Interior Minister says that many male homosexuals choose a profession in hairdressing salons and beauty and massage spas, which the Minister says are often inspected.<sup about="#mwt194" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-gulf-daily-news1_33-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"gulf-daily-news1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-gulf-daily-news1-33" id="mwAW8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXE">[</span>32<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXI">]</span></span></a></sup></li></ul>

<p id="mwAXM">Other pending bills would expressly ban LGBTQ foreigners from entering the kingdom or receiving residency permits as well as plans to instruct children's teachers in apparent warning signs of homosexuality or cross-dressing, so that the children can be punished.<sup about="#mwt197" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-34" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-34"}}'><a href="#cite_note-34" id="mwAXQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXY">[</span>33<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAXg">In September 2013, it was announced that all <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulf_Cooperation_Council" title="Gulf Cooperation Council" id="mwAXk">Gulf Cooperative Countries</a> had agreed to discuss a proposal to establish some form of, yet unknown, testing in order to ban gay foreigners from entering any of the countries.<sup about="#mwt200" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-35" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-35"}}'><a href="#cite_note-35" id="mwAXo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAXs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAXw">[</span>34<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAX0">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt203" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-36" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-36"}}'><a href="#cite_note-36" id="mwAX4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAX8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYA">[</span>35<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYE">]</span></span></a></sup> However, it has been suggested that concern for hosting <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2022 FIFA World Cup" id="mwAYI">2022 FIFA World Cup</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Qatar" title="Qatar" id="mwAYM">Qatar</a>, and fears for controversy in a case that football fans would have been screened, made officials backtrack the plans and insist that it was a mere proposal.<sup about="#mwt206" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-37" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-37"}}'><a href="#cite_note-37" id="mwAYQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAYU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYY">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAYg">In 2018, during its participation in the meeting of the Democracy and Human Rights Committee, the Parliamentary Division of the Kingdom of Bahrain objected to the re-raising of the issue "regarding the role of parliaments in ending discrimination based on sexual orientation, and respecting the rights of gays, bisexuals, transgender people, and intersex persons". The deputy d. Jamila al-Sammak, a member of the Parliamentary Division, said that "this proposal contradicts the natural composition of life, which consists of a man and a woman, just as all monotheistic religions, foremost among which the Islamic religion, have prohibited homosexuality and considered it a departure from the natural scope and the proper formation of life. Therefore, the Division believes that it is necessary to respect the privacy of societies in that matter."<sup about="#mwt209" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Alayam.com_2018_38-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Alayam.com 2018"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Alayam.com_2018-38"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Alayam.com_2018-38" id="mwAYk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAYo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYs">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAYw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAY0">In June 2021, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Embassy_of_the_United_States,_Manama" title="Embassy of the United States, Manama" id="mwAY4">US Embassy</a> and members of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Naval_Support_Activity_Bahrain" title="Naval Support Activity Bahrain" id="mwAY8">US Navy base</a> in Bahrain, celebrated Pride Month by raising the rainbow flag and posting about it on social media,<sup about="#mwt212" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BH_US_Embassy_2021_39-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BH US Embassy 2021"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-BH_US_Embassy_2021-39"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BH_US_Embassy_2021-39" id="mwAZA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZI">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZM">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt215" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Stromko_Bahrain_2022_40-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Stromko Bahrain 2022"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Stromko_Bahrain_2022-40"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Stromko_Bahrain_2022-40" id="mwAZQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZY">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZc">]</span></span></a></sup> prompting <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/State_media" title="State media" id="mwAZg">the state</a>'s <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bahrain_News_Agency" title="Bahrain News Agency" id="mwAZk">Bahrain News Agency</a> (BNA) and all local newspapers to publish several articles in Arabic condemning homosexuality, homosexuals, and even the US embassy.<sup about="#mwt218" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-الصديقي_2021_41-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"الصديقي 2021"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-الصديقي_2021-41"}}'><a href="#cite_note-الصديقي_2021-41" id="mwAZo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZw">[</span>40<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAZ0">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt224" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_Al_42-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA Al"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-BNA_Al-42"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_Al-42" id="mwAZ4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAZ8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaA">[</span>41<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaE">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAaI">On December 6, 2021, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shura_Council_(Bahrain)" title="Shura Council (Bahrain)" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAaM">Shura Council</a> of Bahrain publicly reinforced the country's conservative stance on sexual orientation and gender identity by declaring that homosexuality is contrary to human values and religious principles. This statement aligns with Bahrain's legal framework, which emphasizes personal freedoms while prohibiting behaviours considered immoral, as outlined in the Constitution and the National Action Charter. As a result, LGBTQ individuals in Bahrain face significant legal and social challenges due to these entrenched attitudes and regulations.<sup about="#mwt227" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-43" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-43"}}'><a href="#cite_note-43" id="mwAaQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAaU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaY">[</span>42<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAac">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAag">On February 14, 2024, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Education_(Bahrain)" title="Ministry of Education (Bahrain)" id="mwAak">Ministry of Education in Bahrain</a> decided to refer the administration of a private school and its entire board of directors to investigation. The directive also included the dismissal of the private school's general manager. This decision came in light of a controversial video in which several students performed a scene that included suggestive gestures.<sup about="#mwt230" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:0_44-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":0"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:0-44"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:0-44" id="mwAao"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAas"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAaw">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAa0">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt233" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:1_45-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":1"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:1-45"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:1-45" id="mwAa4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAa8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbA">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbE">]</span></span></a></sup> The scene depicted students enjoying themselves, with two male students pretending to propose to each other in class while other students cheered and had fun. The girl who recorded the video belongs to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Achomi_people" title="Achomi people" id="mwAbI">Achomi</a> ethnic <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ajam_of_Bahrain" title="Ajam of Bahrain" id="mwAbM">minority</a>, whose roots are from <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kuhij" title="Kuhij" id="mwAbQ">Kuhij</a>. Her family is well-known and respected in Bahrain.<sup about="#mwt236" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-46" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-46"}}'><a href="#cite_note-46" id="mwAbU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAbY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbc">[</span>45<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbg">]</span></span></a></sup> The social reaction from the community was overwhelmingly negative and deemed disproportionate.<sup about="#mwt237" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:0_44-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":0"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:0-44" id="mwAbk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAbo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbs">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAbw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt238" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:1_45-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:1-45" id="mwAb0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAb4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAb8">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcA">]</span></span></a></sup> The incident sparked a significant debate on social media and other platforms, raising questions about cultural norms, societal reactions, and the treatment of minority groups in Bahrain.<sup about="#mwt239" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:0_44-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":0"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:0-44" id="mwAcE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAcI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcM">[</span>43<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt240" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:1_45-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":1"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:1-45" id="mwAcU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAcY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcc">[</span>44<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAcg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="10" id="mwAck" aria-labelledby="Living_conditions"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Living_conditions">Living conditions</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=10" title="Edit section: Living conditions"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<section data-mw-section-id="11" id="mwAco" aria-labelledby="Derogatory_terminology"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Derogatory_terminology">Derogatory terminology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=11" title="Edit section: Derogatory terminology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAcs">Arabic publications in Bahrain and the majority of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic" title="Arabic" id="mwAcw">Arabic</a>-speaking <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamic_countries" title="Islamic countries" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAc0">Islamic countries</a>, including laws, commonly refer to homosexuality and homosexuals as:</p>

<ul id="mwAc4"><li id="mwAc8"><b id="mwAdA">Shudhudh</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt241" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAdE" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"شذوذ"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt241">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt241">شذوذ</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt241" id="mwAdI"/>) or <b id="mwAdM">Shudhudh Jinsi</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt242" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAdQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"[[:ar:شذوذ جنسي|شذوذ جنسي]]"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt242">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt242"><a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://ar.wikipedia.org/wiki/شذوذ%20جنسي" title="ar:شذوذ جنسي" class="extiw">شذوذ جنسي</a></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt242" id="mwAdU"/>): used to refer to sexual <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paraphilia" title="Paraphilia" id="mwAdY">paraphilias</a> respectively.<sup about="#mwt243" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-albilad_2022_4-15" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"albilad 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"albilad 2022"},"2":{"wt":"Almaany shududh"},"3":{"wt":"Clemons 2012"},"4":{"wt":"Palmer 2012"},"5":{"wt":"Al-Menbar 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-albilad_2022-4" id="mwAdc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAdg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAdk">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAdo">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt243" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Almaany_shududh_14-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Almaany shududh","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-Almaany_shududh-14" id="mwAds"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAdw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAd0">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAd4">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt243" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Clemons_2012_16-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Clemons 2012","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-Clemons_2012-16" id="mwAd8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAeA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAeE">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAeI">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt243" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Palmer_2012_15-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Palmer 2012","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-Palmer_2012-15" id="mwAeM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAeQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAeU">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAeY">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt243" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Al-Menbar_2022_31-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Al-Menbar 2022","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-Al-Menbar_2022-31" id="mwAec"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAeg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAek">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAeo">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt256" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_2022_Shamsan_47-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref"
