data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA 2022 Shamsan"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-BNA_2022_Shamsan-47"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_2022_Shamsan-47" id="mwAes"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAew"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAe0">[</span>46<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAe4">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt259" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Watan_2022_48-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Watan 2022"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Watan_2022-48"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Watan_2022-48" id="mwAe8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAfA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfE">[</span>47<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfI">]</span></span></a></sup> This is intentionally done to link homosexuals to pedophiles and other paraphilias.<sup about="#mwt262" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-49" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-49"}}'><a href="#cite_note-49" id="mwAfM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAfQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfU">[</span>48<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAfY">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAfc"><b id="mwAfg">Shadh Jinsian</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt263" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAfk" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"شاذ جنسيا"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt263">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt263">شاذ جنسيا</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt263" id="mwAfo"/> – singular), <b id="mwAfs">Shawadh Jinsian</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt264" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAfw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"شواذ جنسيا"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt264">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt264">شواذ جنسيا</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt264" id="mwAf0"/> – plural): meaning sexual paraphilic(s).</li>
<li id="mwAf4"><b id="mwAf8">Shadh</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt265" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAgA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"شاذ"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt265">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt265">شاذ</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt265" id="mwAgE"/> – singular), and <b id="mwAgI">Shawadh</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt266" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAgM" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"شواذ"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt266">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt266">شواذ</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt266" id="mwAgQ"/> – plural): meaning paraphilic(s), pervert(s) or deviant(s).</li></ul>
<p id="mwAgU">These terms denote "sexual paraphilia, perversion or deviation" such as <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pedophilia" title="Pedophilia" id="mwAgY">pedophilia</a>;<sup about="#mwt267" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Almaany_shududh_14-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Almaany shududh","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Almaany shududh"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-Almaany_shududh-14" id="mwAgc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAgg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgk">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgo">]</span></span></a></sup> when used to refer to "homosexuals" they have derogatory and dehumanizing connotations.<sup about="#mwt270" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Palmer_2012_15-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Palmer 2012","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Palmer 2012"},"2":{"wt":"Clemons 2012"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-Palmer_2012-15" id="mwAgs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAgw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAg0">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAg4">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt270" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Clemons_2012_16-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Clemons 2012","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-Clemons_2012-16" id="mwAg8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAhA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhE">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhI">]</span></span></a></sup>
Other derogatory terms include:</p>
<ul id="mwAhM"><li id="mwAhQ"><b id="mwAhU">Lewat</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt275" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAhY" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"لواط"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt275">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt275">لواط</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt275" id="mwAhc"/>)- equivalent to "<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sodomy" title="Sodomy" id="mwAhg">sodomy</a>", in reference to the Biblical and Quranic figure <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lot_(biblical_person)" title="Lot (biblical person)" id="mwAhk">Lot</a>.<sup about="#mwt276" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Palmer_2012_15-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Palmer 2012","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Palmer 2012"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-Palmer_2012-15" id="mwAho"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAhs"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAhw">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAh0">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAh4"><b id="mwAh8">Luti</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt279" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAiA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"لوطي"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt279">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt279">لوطي</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt279" id="mwAiE"/>) – equivalent to "sodomite."<sup about="#mwt280" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Palmer_2012_15-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Palmer 2012","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Palmer 2012"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-Palmer_2012-15" id="mwAiI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAiM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAiQ">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAiU">]</span></span></a></sup></li></ul>
<p id="mwAiY">In contrast, non-derogatory terms are available, like:</p>
<ul id="mwAic"><li id="mwAig"><b id="mwAik">Mithleyya Jinsia</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt283" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAio" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"[[:ar:مثلية جنسية|مثلية جنسية]]"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt283">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt283"><a rel="mw:WikiLink/Interwiki" href="https://ar.wikipedia.org/wiki/مثلية%20جنسية" title="ar:مثلية جنسية" class="extiw">مثلية جنسية</a></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt283" id="mwAis"/>) which exclusively means <i id="mwAiw">homosexuality</i>.<sup about="#mwt284" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Palmer_2012_15-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Palmer 2012","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"Palmer 2012"},"2":{"wt":"Clemons 2012"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-Palmer_2012-15" id="mwAi0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAi4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAi8">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAjA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt284" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Clemons_2012_16-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Clemons 2012","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-Clemons_2012-16" id="mwAjE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAjI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAjM">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAjQ">]</span></span></a></sup></li>
<li id="mwAjU"><b id="mwAjY">Mithley-yeen</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt289" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAjc" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"مثليين"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt289">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt289">مثليين</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt289" id="mwAjg"/>) which exclusively means <i id="mwAjk">homosexuals</i>.</li>
<li id="mwAjo"><b id="mwAjs">Mithley</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt290" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAjw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"مثلي"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt290">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt290">مثلي</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt290" id="mwAj0"/>) which means <i id="mwAj4">gay</i>.</li>
<li id="mwAj8"><b id="mwAkA">Mithleya</b> (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt291" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAkE" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"مثلية"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt291">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt291">مثلية</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt291" id="mwAkI"/>) which means <i id="mwAkM">lesbian</i>.</li></ul>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="12" id="mwAkQ" aria-labelledby="Incidents_and_enforcement"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Incidents_and_enforcement">Incidents and enforcement</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=12" title="Edit section: Incidents and enforcement"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwAkU">A government crackdown against cross-dressing appears to have begun a year after the 2008 call by members of the Al Menbar parliamentary bloc for harsher action on same-sex behaviour. In 2009, two Asian foreigners were sentenced to six months in jail, with hard labour and later deportation, for offering to have sex with undercover police offices in exchange for money at a male barbershop.<sup about="#mwt298" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-50" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-50"}}'><a href="#cite_note-50" id="mwAkY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAkc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAkg">[</span>49<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAkk">]</span></span></a></sup> In February 2009, a 39-year-old man was sentenced to a month in jail for wearing women's clothing in public, namely an <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abaya" title="Abaya" id="mwAko">abaya</a> and purse.<sup about="#mwt301" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-51" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-51"}}'><a href="#cite_note-51" id="mwAks"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAkw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAk0">[</span>50<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAk4">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAk8">In 2011, police raided a party that was described in the press as a same-sex wedding ceremony.<sup about="#mwt304" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-52" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-52"}}'><a href="#cite_note-52" id="mwAlA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAlE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlI">[</span>51<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlM">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt307" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-U.S._2011_53-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"U.S. 2011"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-U.S._2011-53"}}'><a href="#cite_note-U.S._2011-53" id="mwAlQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAlU"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlY">[</span>52<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAlc">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="13" id="mwAlg" aria-labelledby="False_accusations"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="False_accusations">False accusations</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=13" title="Edit section: False accusations"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwAlk">Sometimes false accusations of homosexuality, or anti-gay innuendos, are levied against critics of the Bahraini government in an effort to discredit political or sectarian dissent. In a society where being gay is widely perceived as a vice or bad habit, accusing people of being gay, even in jest, can be an effective means of bullying reformers.</p>

<p id="mwAlo">Human rights advocate Nabeel Rajab has been subjected to such accusations by people on social media. Similar insults have been launched at Sheikh Ali Salman, with some Twitter users referring to his Shia political party as "Al Wefag".<sup about="#mwt310" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-al-bab.com_54-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"al-bab.com"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-al-bab.com-54"}}'><a href="#cite_note-al-bab.com-54" id="mwAls"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAlw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAl0">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAl4">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAl8">Similarly, false accusations were circulated about the 2011 pro-democracy protesters gathered in Pearl Square. Participants of the protests were accused of engaging in all sorts of sexual immorality in an effort to discredit the protesters' demands.<sup about="#mwt311" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-al-bab.com_54-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"al-bab.com"}}'><a href="#cite_note-al-bab.com-54" id="mwAmA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAmE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmI">[</span>53<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="14" id="mwAmQ" aria-labelledby="Public_opinion_and_media_coverage"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Public_opinion_and_media_coverage">Public opinion and media coverage</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=14" title="Edit section: Public opinion and media coverage"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<p id="mwAmg">According to the World Values Survey in 2011, 42% of Bahraini people believed that "homosexuality is never justified", which was lower than the world average of 48% who agreed with that statement. It was also more accepting than any other Arab countries surveyed. The same survey found that 18% of Bahraini people "would not like to have homosexuals as neighbours" which was among the lowest percentage in the world.<sup about="#mwt318" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-55" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-55"}}'><a href="#cite_note-55" id="mwAmk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAmo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAms">[</span>54<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAmw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAm0">Some of the more liberal and left-aligned lawful political groups within Bahrain have expressed opposition to introducing Sharia law into the Bahraini penal code, but none have expressed support for LGBTQ rights.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt319" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAm4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Citation needed","href":"./Template:Citation_needed"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"November 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_November_2022" about="#mwt319"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt319" id="mwAm8"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (November 2022)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

<p id="mwAnA">In June 2021, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_diplomatic_missions_of_the_United_States" title="List of diplomatic missions of the United States" id="mwAnE">United States Embassy in Bahrain</a> faced public criticism following its decision to raise the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rainbow_flag_(LGBTQ)" title="Rainbow flag (LGBTQ)" id="mwAnI">rainbow flag</a> in recognition of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pride_Month" title="Pride Month" id="mwAnM">LGBTQ Pride Month</a>. In Bahrain, as in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab_states_of_the_Persian_Gulf" title="Arab states of the Persian Gulf" id="mwAnQ">other Gulf states</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Homosexuality" title="Homosexuality" id="mwAnU">homosexuality</a> remains a highly sensitive and controversial subject. The flag's display prompted members of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Assembly_(Bahrain)" title="National Assembly (Bahrain)" id="mwAnY">Bahraini National Assembly</a> to issue a statement condemning the act, describing it as a "provocative action".<sup about="#mwt322" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-56" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-56"}}'><a href="#cite_note-56" id="mwAnc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAng"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnk">[</span>55<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAno">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAns">In December 2021, the President of the Bahraini Educators Union, Safia Shamsan, described the statement of the Supreme Council for Islamic Affairs and its support for the statement of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Azhar_al-Sharif" title="Al-Azhar al-Sharif" id="mwAnw">Al-Azhar</a> as "a right stance that we all support". And she explained that "what is constant in the laws of the earth is the common sense that <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Allah" title="Allah" id="mwAn0">Allah</a> instilled in us and that is reinforced and protected by the heavenly religions". Shamsan claimed that it is "important to stand against the issue of <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt323" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAn4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"alshudhudh aljinsi"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">alshudhudh aljinsi</i></span> [homosexuality] and preventing its promotion through a systematic and fraudulent media".<sup about="#mwt324" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_2022_Shamsan_47-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA 2022 Shamsan"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_2022_Shamsan-47" id="mwAn8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAoA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoE">[</span>46<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoI">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAoM">In the same year, Ma'an (<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt325" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAoQ" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"معاً"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt325">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt325">معاً</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt325" id="mwAoU"/>), a Bahraini <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_rights" title="Human rights" id="mwAoY">human rights</a> organization, issued an article on BNA in Arabic titled "We support the statement of the Islamic Supreme Council and call for respect for Islamic and societal values", calling for "protection of society from all manifestations of <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt326" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAoc" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"Shudhudh"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">Shudhudh</i></span> (sexual deviance) and attacks on Islamic and societal principles and values."<sup about="#mwt327" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_Maan_2021_2-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA Maan 2021"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_Maan_2021-2" id="mwAog"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAok"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoo">[</span>2<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAos">]</span></span></a></sup> BNA and the local Bahraini newspapers accused Qatar of "promoting homosexuality" in the then upcoming <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_World_Cup" title="FIFA World Cup" id="mwAow">FIFA World Cup</a>.<sup about="#mwt330" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Alayam.com_2022_57-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Alayam.com 2022"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Alayam.com_2022-57"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Alayam.com_2022-57" id="mwAo0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAo4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAo8">[</span>56<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwApA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt333" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_2022_Anti-Qatar_propaganda_58-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA 2022 Anti-Qatar propaganda"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-BNA_2022_Anti-Qatar_propaganda-58"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_2022_Anti-Qatar_propaganda-58" id="mwApE"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwApI"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwApM">[</span>57<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwApQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwApU">In June 2022, coinciding with pride month again, the US Embassy raised a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rainbow_flag_(LGBTQ)" title="Rainbow flag (LGBTQ)" id="mwApY">rainbow flag</a> and celebrated pride on their social media accounts, which instigated <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bahrain_News_Agency" title="Bahrain News Agency" id="mwApc">BNA</a> and all local government allied newspapers (including <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Ayam_(Bahrain)" title="Al Ayam (Bahrain)" id="mwApg"><i id="mwApk">Al-Ayam</i></a>; a self-identified <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Liberalism" title="Liberalism" id="mwApo">liberal</a> newspaper) to publish several articles (in Arabic) condemning, attacking homosexuals (and labeling them as "<span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt334" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAps" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"Shawadh"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">Shawadh</i></span>" lit: 'paraphilic', 'perverted' or 'deviants'), and attacking the US Embassy itself, and claiming that <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Homosexuality_and_religion" title="Homosexuality and religion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwApw">"all religions" are against homosexuality</a>, in addition to pseudoscientific Anti-Gay propaganda by saying no one is born gay and portraying <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Monkeypox" title="Monkeypox" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAp0">monkeypox</a> as a "Gay Plague".<sup about="#mwt341" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-BNA_2022_Pride_Response_3-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"BNA 2022 Pride Response"}}'><a href="#cite_note-BNA_2022_Pride_Response-3" id="mwAp4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAp8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqA">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt342" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Watan_2022_48-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Watan 2022"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Watan_2022-48" id="mwAqI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAqM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqQ">[</span>47<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqU">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt351" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-الشيخ_2022_59-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"الشيخ 2022"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-الشيخ_2022-59"}}'><a href="#cite_note-الشيخ_2022-59" id="mwAqY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAqc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqg">[</span>58<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAqk">]</span></span></a></sup> In contrast, while a <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sunni_Islam" title="Sunni Islam" id="mwAqo">Sunni</a> woman who accused <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Shia_Islam" title="Shia Islam" id="mwAqs">Shia</a> Muslims of being responsible for the spread of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/COVID-19" title="COVID-19" id="mwAqw">COVID-19</a> was arrested,<sup about="#mwt354" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-irf_report_60-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"irf_report"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-irf_report-60"}}'><a href="#cite_note-irf_report-60" id="mwAq0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAq4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAq8">[</span>59<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArA">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hate_speech" title="Hate speech" id="mwArE">hate speech</a> against the LGBTQ+ community remains widespread, with no legal consequences for those who engage in such rhetoric.<sup about="#mwt335" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-insta_61-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"insta","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"insta"},"2":{"wt":"tariq"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-insta-61" id="mwArI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwArM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArQ">[</span>60<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArU">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt335" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-tariq_62-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"tariq","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a href="#cite_note-tariq-62" id="mwArY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwArc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArg">[</span>61<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwArk">]</span></span></a></sup><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_failed_verification" about="#mwt340" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAro" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"failed verification","href":"./Template:Failed_verification"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"January 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" verification of its source citation(s). (January 2025)">failed verification</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup> This is partly due to Bahrain's Article 309, which protects religious groups but does not extend similar protections to the LGBTQ+ community.<sup about="#mwt358" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Freedom_of_Thought_Report_20192_63-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Freedom of Thought Report 20192"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Freedom_of_Thought_Report_20192-63"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Freedom_of_Thought_Report_20192-63" id="mwArw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAr0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAr4">[</span>62<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAr8">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt361" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-end_blasphemy_64-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"end_blasphemy"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-end_blasphemy-64"}}'><a href="#cite_note-end_blasphemy-64" id="mwAsA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAsE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsI">[</span>63<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAsM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAsQ">On November 15, 2022, a Bahraini <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/YouTube" title="YouTube" id="mwAsU">YouTube</a> talk show <i id="mwAsY">Speaker bh</i> hosted an episode about homosexuals titled "<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic_language" title="Arabic language" about="#mwt362" typeof="mw:Transclusion" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAsc" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"الشذوذ الجنسي ( المثليين )"},"4":{"wt":"Sexual deviance (homosexuals)"}},"i":0}}]}'>Arabic</a><span about="#mwt362">: </span><span lang="ar" dir="rtl" about="#mwt362">الشذوذ الجنسي ( المثليين )</span><span about="#mwt362">, </span><small about="#mwt362"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Literal_translation" title="Literal translation">lit.</a><span typeof="mw:Entity"> </span></small><span class="gloss-quot" about="#mwt362">'</span><span class="gloss-text" about="#mwt362">Sexual deviance (homosexuals)</span><span class="gloss-quot" about="#mwt362">'</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt362" id="mwAsg"/>" where the episode featured two hosts, <span title="Arabic-language text" about="#mwt363" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAsk" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"محمد القطان"},"4":{"wt":"Mohammed Al-Qattan"},"label":{"wt":"none"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="ar" dir="rtl">محمد القطان</span></span><span about="#mwt363">, </span><span class="gloss-quot" about="#mwt363">'</span><span class="gloss-text" about="#mwt363">Mohammed Al-Qattan</span><span class="gloss-quot" about="#mwt363">'</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt363" id="mwAso"/>, and <span title="Arabic-language text" about="#mwt364" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAss" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"سمر الزرقاوي"},"4":{"wt":"Samar Al-Zarqawi"},"label":{"wt":"none"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="ar" dir="rtl">سمر الزرقاوي</span></span><span about="#mwt364">, </span><span class="gloss-quot" about="#mwt364">'</span><span class="gloss-text" about="#mwt364">Samar Al-Zarqawi</span><span class="gloss-quot" about="#mwt364">'</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt364" id="mwAsw"/> who used mockery and slang expressions while discussing the topic, saying:<sup about="#mwt367" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-speak_pod_65-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"speak pod"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-speak_pod-65"}}'><a href="#cite_note-speak_pod-65" id="mwAs0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAs4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAs8">[</span>64<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1244412712" about="#mwt368" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwAtE" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"text":{"wt":"انه اوه قي هاهاها عادي، لا لا لا مو عادي مو حرية شخصية ما يصير، يعني احنا في مجتمع اسلامي، هاي ضد الفطرة، احنا تربينا على مبدأ الفطرة مثلاً الريال لازم يتزوج و اييب عيال و كذا و كذا ما يصير{{pb}}[That it&apos;s like, &apos;Oh, Gay, haha, it&apos;s normal.&apos; No, no, no, it&apos;s not normal. It&apos;s not personal freedom; it can&apos;t be. I mean, we live in an Islamic society. This goes against human nature. We were raised on the principle of natural order. For example, a man must marry, have children, and so on. It can&apos;t be like this.]"}},"i":0}}]}'/><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt368" id="mwAtI"><p>انه اوه قي هاهاها عادي، لا لا لا مو عادي مو حرية شخصية ما يصير، يعني احنا في مجتمع اسلامي، هاي ضد الفطرة، احنا تربينا على مبدأ الفطرة مثلاً الريال لازم يتزوج و اييب عيال و كذا و كذا ما يصير</p><div class="paragraphbreak" style="margin-top:0.5em"></div><p>[That it's like, 'Oh, Gay, haha, it's normal.' No, no, no, it's not normal. It's not personal freedom; it can't be. I mean, we live in an Islamic society. This goes against human nature. We were raised on the principle of natural order. For example, a man must marry, have children, and so on. It can't be like this.]</p></blockquote><p id="mwAtM"> The co-host herself does not abide by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hijab#Qur'an" title="Hijab" id="mwAtQ">Islamic dress code</a>.<sup about="#mwt370" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-speak_pod_65-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"speak pod","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"speak pod"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-speak_pod-65" id="mwAtU"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAtY"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtc">[</span>64<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAtg">]</span></span></a></sup> The host was criticised in the comments for hosting what they called an "<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Effeminate" title="Effeminate" class="mw-redirect" id="mwAtk">effeminate</a>" known as "<span title="Arabic-language text" about="#mwt373" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAto" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Langx","href":"./Template:Langx"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"جوزيف"},"4":{"wt":"Joseph"},"label":{"wt":"none"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="ar" dir="rtl">جوزيف</span></span><span about="#mwt373">, </span><span class="gloss-quot" about="#mwt373">'</span><span class="gloss-text" about="#mwt373">Joseph</span><span class="gloss-quot" about="#mwt373">'</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt373" id="mwAts"/>" a popular Bahraini <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snapchat" title="Snapchat" id="mwAtw">snapchat</a> persona in the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arab_states_of_the_Persian_Gulf" title="Arab states of the Persian Gulf" id="mwAt0">Arab Gulf state</a>.<sup about="#mwt374" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-speak_pod_65-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"speak pod","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"speak pod"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-speak_pod-65" id="mwAt4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAt8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuA">[</span>64<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuE">]</span></span></a></sup> Media content in Bahrain is subject to government oversight, with only content aligning with state-approved narratives being permitted for publication.<sup about="#mwt379" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-66" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-66"}}'><a href="#cite_note-66" id="mwAuI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAuM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuQ">[</span>65<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAuY">Similarly, in June 2023, Salah Al-Jodar, a columnist for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Ayam_(Bahrain)" title="Al Ayam (Bahrain)" id="mwAuc">Al-Ayam</a> newspaper, published an article strongly condemning the promotion of LGBTQ+ rights in Bahrain.<sup about="#mwt381" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_20-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-20" id="mwAug"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAuk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAuo">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAus">]</span></span></a></sup> He criticized what he described as Western moral decline, linking issues such as family disintegration, divorce, and homelessness to the acceptance of homosexuality.<sup about="#mwt382" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_20-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-20" id="mwAuw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAu0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAu4">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAu8">]</span></span></a></sup> Al-Jodar argued that Western societies, having lost touch with traditional values, are "attempting to <i id="mwAvA">export these ideas</i> to the Arab world under the guise of personal freedom" (despite the fact that <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LGBTQ_history#Middle_East_and_North_Africa" title="LGBTQ history" id="mwAvE">homosexuality existed in the Middle East</a> before modern gay rights).<sup about="#mwt383" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_20-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-20" id="mwAvI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAvM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvQ">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvU">]</span></span></a></sup> Al-Jodar's article was written in response to the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_diplomatic_missions_of_the_United_States" title="List of diplomatic missions of the United States" id="mwAvY">US Embassy in Bahrain</a>'s public support for LGBTQ+ rights during <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pride_Month" title="Pride Month" id="mwAvc">Pride Month</a>.<sup about="#mwt384" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_20-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-20" id="mwAvg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAvk"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvo">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvs">]</span></span></a></sup> He expressed disapproval of the embassy's actions, stating that such behavior disrespects Bahrain's customs, traditions, and religious values.<sup about="#mwt385" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_20-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-20" id="mwAvw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAv0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAv4">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAv8">]</span></span></a></sup> He claimed that Bahraini society firmly rejects homosexuality, which he referred to as <span title="Arabic-language romanization" about="#mwt380" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAwA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Transliteration","href":"./Template:Transliteration"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"shawadh"}},"i":0}}]}'><i lang="ar-Latn">shawadh</i></span> ('<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deviance_(sociology)" title="Deviance (sociology)" id="mwAwE">deviant</a>'), and maintained that all religions and societal norms oppose such behaviours.<sup about="#mwt386" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_20-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-20" id="mwAwI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAwM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAwQ">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAwU">]</span></span></a></sup> Al-Jodar's rhetoric mirrors common arguments in Bahrain, where LGBTQ+ identities are frequently portrayed as foreign to the country's social fabric and religious principles.<sup about="#mwt387" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_20-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-20" id="mwAwY"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAwc"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAwg">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAwk">]</span></span></a></sup> His views also reflect broader societal attitudes in Bahrain, where public expressions of support for LGBTQ+ rights often face significant backlash, particularly from conservative and religious groups. Despite growing awareness of LGBTQ+ issues globally, public opinion in Bahrain remains largely hostile, with social media platforms often serving as outlets for criticism against LGBTQ+ rights, especially during Pride Month.<sup about="#mwt388" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:7_20-8" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:7-20" id="mwAwo"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAws"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAww">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAw0">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAw4">In September 2023, Sonya Janahi, a member of the Bahrain Chamber of Commerce and Industry <span class="failed-verification-content" style="padding-left:0.1em; padding-right:0.1em; color:var(--color-subtle, #54595d); border:1px solid var(--border-color-subtle, #c8ccd1);" about="#mwt389" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAw8" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"failed verification span","href":"./Template:Failed_verification_span"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"and part of [[Ajam of Bahrain|Bahrain&apos;s Ajam community]],"},"date":{"wt":"January 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'>and part of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ajam_of_Bahrain" title="Ajam of Bahrain">Bahrain's Ajam community</a>,</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_failed_verification" about="#mwt389"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_failed_verification_from_January_2025" about="#mwt389"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="margin-left:0.1em; white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt389" id="mwAxA"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability"><span title="The material highlighted is not verifiable using the citation(s) presented. (January 2025)">failed verification</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_that_may_have_off-topic_sections" about="#mwt390" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAxE" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Relevance inline","href":"./Template:Relevance_inline"},"params":{"reason":{"wt":"Why is Janahi&apos;s ethnicity relevant to her views on this topic?"},"date":{"wt":"January 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Wikipedia_articles_that_may_have_off-topic_sections_from_January_2025" about="#mwt390"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template " style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt390" id="mwAxI"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Writing_better_articles#Stay_on_topic" title="Wikipedia:Writing better articles"><span title="The material near this tag may contain information that is not relevant to the article's main topic. Why is Janahi's ethnicity relevant to her views on this topic? (January 2025)">relevant?</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup> spoke at the Arab Chambers Union meeting in Manama.<sup about="#mwt391" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_alblilad_67-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 alblilad","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"2023 alblilad"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_alblilad-67" id="mwAxM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAxQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxU">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxY">]</span></span></a></sup> During the meeting, Janahi voiced her opposition to the acceptance of homosexuality, stating that it conflicts with Islamic and societal values.<sup about="#mwt394" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_alblilad_67-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 alblilad","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"2023 alblilad"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_alblilad-67" id="mwAxc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAxg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxk">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAxo">]</span></span></a></sup> She urged regional governments to form a unified stance on this issue, particularly within the Arab Labour Organization.<sup about="#mwt397" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_alblilad_67-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 alblilad","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"2023 alblilad"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_alblilad-67" id="mwAxs"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAxw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAx0">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAx4">]</span></span></a></sup> Janahi pointed out that discussions on sexual orientation and gender identity had led to divisions during the organization's financial committee meetings.<sup about="#mwt400" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_alblilad_67-3" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 alblilad","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"2023 alblilad"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_alblilad-67" id="mwAx8"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAyA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyE">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyI">]</span></span></a></sup> Despite efforts to resolve these disagreements, she noted that many Arab governments continue to resist incorporating issues related to homosexuality into official discussions.<sup about="#mwt403" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_alblilad_67-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Transclusion mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 alblilad","group":""},"body":{"html":""},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"r","href":"./Template:R"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"2023 alblilad"}},"i":0}}]}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_alblilad-67" id="mwAyM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAyQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyU">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyY">]</span></span></a></sup> Janahi further mentioned that around 50% of the member countries in the Arab League oppose homosexuality.<sup about="#mwt408" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_alblilad_67-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 alblilad"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-2023_alblilad-67"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_alblilad-67" id="mwAyc"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAyg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyk">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAyo">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAys">In February 2024, Ibrahim Al-Nahham, a Bahraini journalist working for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Bilad_(Bahraini_newspaper)" title="Al Bilad (Bahraini newspaper)" id="mwAyw"><i id="mwAy0">Al-Bilad</i></a>, published an article titled "Homosexuality and the Sinister Plot" (<span title="Arabic-language text" about="#mwt409" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAy4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"المثلية الجنسية والمخطط الآثم"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="ar" dir="rtl">المثلية الجنسية والمخطط الآثم</span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt409" id="mwAy8"/>), expressing conservative attitudes towards LGBTQ individuals. Al-Nahham strongly condemned homosexuality, labelling it as a threat to the country's moral fabric, religious values, and family structure. He expressed concerns about global media "promoting same-sex relationships", arguing that such portrayals undermine traditional Arab-Islamic values and encourage what he described as "deviant" behaviours. The article called for the Bahraini parliament to take firm action by criminalizing homosexual acts and same-sex marriage to safeguard societal norms and protect future generations. This perspective aligns with the broader public sentiment in Bahrain, where homosexuality is widely viewed as incompatible with the country's cultural and religious foundations.<sup about="#mwt412" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-68" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-68"}}'><a href="#cite_note-68" id="mwAzA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAzE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAzI">[</span>67<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAzM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

posted an illustration drawn by <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Caricature" title="Caricature" id="mwAzo">caricature</a> artist Tariq Al-Bahhar on their website and on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Instagram" title="Instagram" id="mwAzs">Instagram</a> depicting a roll of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toilet_paper" title="Toilet paper" id="mwAzw">toilet paper</a> with the colours of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rainbow_flag_(LGBTQ)" title="Rainbow flag (LGBTQ)" id="mwAz0">Gay pride flag</a>, accompanied by the caption "<span title="Arabic-language text" about="#mwt413" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwAz4" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"lang","href":"./Template:Lang"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"ar"},"2":{"wt":"المكان الصحيح"}},"i":0}}]}'><span lang="ar" dir="rtl">المكان الصحيح</span></span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_Arabic-language_text" about="#mwt413" id="mwAz8"/>" ('the correct place').<sup about="#mwt414" class="mw-ref about="#mwt414" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-tariq_62-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"tariq","group":""},"body":{"html":""}}'><a caricature, which was widely viewed and commented on,<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt419" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwA0g" Such content reinforces [[Social stigma|societal stigmas]] and hostility, contributing to the broader atmosphere of intolerance that LGBTQ+ individuals face in the country."},"date":{"wt":"January 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'>reflects the prevailing negative attitudes towards the LGBTQ+ community in Bahrain. Such content reinforces <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Social_stigma" title="Social stigma">societal stigmas</a> and hostility, contributing to the broader atmosphere of intolerance that LGBTQ+ individuals face in the country.</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt420"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_January_2025" about="#mwt420"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="margin-left:0.1em; white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt420" id="mwA0s"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (January 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup> Public expressions like this are <span class="citation-needed-content" style="padding-left:0.1em; padding-right:0.1em; color:var(--color-subtle, #54595d); border:1px solid var(--border-color-subtle, #c8ccd1);" about="#mwt421" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwA0w" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"citation needed span","href":"./Template:Citation_needed_span"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"emblematic of the broader [[conservative]] and religious sentiments that shape attitudes toward LGBTQ+ rights in the region."},"date":{"wt":"January 2025"}},"i":0}}]}'>emblematic of the broader <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conservative" title="Conservative" class="mw-redirect">conservative</a> and religious sentiments that shape attitudes toward LGBTQ+ rights in the region.</span><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt421"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_January_2025" about="#mwt421"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="margin-left:0.1em; white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt421" id="mwA00"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (January 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="15" id="mwA04" aria-labelledby="Free_speech_and_association"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Free_speech_and_association">Free speech and association</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=15" title="Edit section: Free speech and association"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA08">The press in Bahrain has, since the 1990s, generally been allowed to discuss the subject of homosexuality, without being punished by the government. Initially, the discussion was focused on people and events happening outside of Bahrain, especially in the field of entertainment or the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/AIDS" title="AIDS" class="mw-redirect" id="mwA1A">AIDS</a>-<a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HIV" title="HIV" id="mwA1E">HIV</a> pandemic. In the early part of the twenty-first century, the Bahraini press has begun to address <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sexual_orientation" title="Sexual orientation" id="mwA1I">sexual orientation</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gender_identity" title="Gender identity" id="mwA1M">gender identity</a>, and the AIDS-HIV pandemic as they apply to the island.</p>

<p id="mwA1Q">In 2001, the Arabic language newspaper <i id="mwA1U"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al-Meethaq?action=edit&amp;redlink=1" title="Al-Meethaq (page does not exist)" class="new" typeof="mw:LocalizedAttrs" id="mwA1Y" data-mw-i18n='{"title":{"lang":"x-page","key":"red-link-title","params":["Al-Meethaq"]}}'>Al-Meethaq</a></i> created a national controversy when it became the first newspaper to discuss homosexuality in Bahrain.<sup about="#mwt424" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-69" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-69"}}'><a href="#cite_note-69" id="mwA1c"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA1g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA1k">[</span>68<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA1o">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwA1s">On 21 December 2005, the Bahrain-based newspaper <i id="mwA1w"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulf_Daily_News" title="Gulf Daily News" id="mwA10">Gulf Daily News</a>'</i> British columnist <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Les_Horton_(columnist)?action=edit&amp;redlink=1" title="Les Horton (columnist) (page does not exist)" class="new" typeof="mw:LocalizedAttrs" id="mwA14" data-mw-i18n='{"title":{"lang":"x-page","key":"red-link-title","params":["Les Horton (columnist)"]}}'>Les Horton</a> wrote a commentary.<sup about="#mwt427" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-70" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-70"}}'><a href="#cite_note-70" id="mwA18"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA2A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2E">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2I">]</span></span></a></sup> This is probably the first time that a column expressing support for LGBTQ rights was published in a Bahraini newspaper, albeit an English language publication.</p>

<p id="mwA2M">Bahraini citizens who criticize or denigrate Islamic scripture, including advocating for a more permissive interpretation, can be imprisoned for up to one year. An unidentified female Bahraini citizen who mocked Islamic scripture on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Twitter" title="Twitter" class="mw-redirect" id="mwA2Q">Twitter</a>, was reported by people and this got her subsequently arrested.<sup about="#mwt431" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-https://www.bna.bh/_71-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"https://www.bna.bh/"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-https://www.bna.bh/-71"}}'><a href="#cite_note-https://www.bna.bh/-71" id="mwA2U"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA2Y"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2c">[</span>70<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2g">]</span></span></a></sup> The <i id="mwA2k">Gulf Daily News</i> has continued to write articles that touch upon homosexuality and gender identity. For example, it has published several articles on Bahraini female homosexuality in girls' high schools and Bahraini women who claim to have become lesbians based on abusive relationships with men.<link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_unsourced_statements" about="#mwt428" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwA2o" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Citation needed","href":"./Template:Citation_needed"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"November 2022"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_unsourced_statements_from_November_2022" about="#mwt428"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt428" id="mwA2s"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (November 2022)">citation needed</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup></p>

<p id="mwA2w"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Human_Rights_Watch" title="Human Rights Watch" id="mwA20">Human Rights Watch</a>, the international non-governmental organisation, has identified legal and social barriers to the formation of community groups or associations for support or promotion of LGBTQ rights in Bahrain.<sup about="#mwt434" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-72" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-72"}}'><a href="#cite_note-72" id="mwA24"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA28"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3A">[</span>71<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3E">]</span></span></a></sup> The government body that controls the registration of associations and private foundations may deny registration to any organisation if it judges that its aims are contrary to morality or the maintenance of public order, or if "society does not need its services".<sup about="#mwt437" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ilga_2020_11-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ilga 2020"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-ilga_2020-11"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ilga_2020-11" id="mwA3I"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA3M"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3Q">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3U">]</span></span></a></sup>
The US Department of State reports there are no approved LGBTQ-related organisations and deems it unlikely they could form, or "openly convene events publicly supportive of the LGBTQI+ community" in the face of currently prevailing societal norms. ILGA concludes that given the social and legal context, it is "improbable that an organisation working on sexual and gender diversity issues would be registered".<sup about="#mwt440" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_ussd_6-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 ussd"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-2023_ussd-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_ussd-6" id="mwA3Y"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA3c"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3g">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3k">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="16" id="mwA3o" aria-labelledby="Human_rights_reports"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Human_rights_reports">Human rights reports</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=16" title="Edit section: Human rights reports"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p id="mwA3s">In 2017, the United States Department of State reported the following, concerning the status of LGBTQ rights in Bahrain:<sup about="#mwt443" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-state_2017_12-2" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"state 2017"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-state_2017-12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-state_2017-12" id="mwA3w"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA30"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA34">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA38">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1244412712" about="#mwt444" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwA4A" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"The law does not criminalize same-sex sexual activity between consenting persons who are at least age 21, but discrimination based on sexual orientation or gender identity occurred."}},"i":0}}]}'/><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt444" id="mwA4E"><p>The law does not criminalize same-sex sexual activity between consenting persons who are at least age 21, but discrimination based on sexual orientation or gender identity occurred.</p></blockquote><p id="mwA4I">The 2023 Country Human Rights report from the US Department of State said:<sup about="#mwt448" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_ussd_6-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 ussd"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_ussd-6" id="mwA4M"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA4Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4U">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4Y">]</span></span></a></sup></p><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1244412712" about="#mwt446" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles mw:Transclusion" id="mwA4c" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Blockquote/styles.css"},"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"blockquote","href":"./Template:Blockquote"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"The law did not criminalize same-sex sexual conduct between consenting adults. It prescribed fines, imprisonment, deportation, or a combination thereof for persons engaging in \"debauchery\" or \"public indecency\". The government used this provision against individuals suspected of being lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender, queer, or intersex (LGBTQI+) or of \"cross-dressing\"."}},"i":0}}]}'/><blockquote class="templatequote " about="#mwt446" id="mwA4g"><p>The law did not criminalize same-sex sexual conduct between consenting adults. It prescribed fines, imprisonment, deportation, or a combination thereof for persons engaging in "debauchery" or "public indecency". The government used this provision against individuals suspected of being lesbian, gay, bisexual, transgender, queer, or intersex (LGBTQI+) or of "cross-dressing".</p></blockquote>

<td id="mwA8U">Recognition of same-sex couples</td>
<td id="mwA8Y"><span typeof="mw:File" id="mwA8c" data-mw='{"caption":"No"}'><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:X_mark.svg" class="mw-file-description" title="No" id="mwA8g"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:X_mark.svg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a2/X_mark.svg/20px-X_mark.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a2/X_mark.svg/40px-X_mark.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="No" data-file-width="525" data-file-height="600" data-file-type="drawing" height="17" width="15" class="mw-file-element" id="mwA8k"/></a></span></td></tr>
<tr id="mwA8o">
<td id="mwA8s">Step-child adoption by same-sex couples</td>
<td id="mwA8w"><span typeof="mw:File" id="mwA80" data-mw='{"caption":"No"}'><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:X_mark.svg" class="mw-file-description" title="No" id="mwA84"><img resource="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:X_mark.svg" src="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a2/X_mark.svg/20px-X_mark.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail" decoding="async" loading="lazy" srcset="//thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a2/X_mark.svg/40px-X_mark.svg.png?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=parser&amp;utm_content=thumbnail 2x" alt="No" data-file-width="525" data-file-height="600" data-file-type="drawing" height="17" width="15" class="mw-file-element" id="mwA88"/></a></span> Adoption is not legally available to anyone, regardless of sex or orientation, in accordance with traditional interpretations of Islamic law regarding family relationships<sup about="#mwt460" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-kbp_uk_73-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"kbp uk"}}'><a href="#cite_note-kbp_uk-73" id="mwA9A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA9E"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA9I">[</span>72<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA9M">]</span></span></a></sup></td></tr>

<td id="mwA-U">Right to change legal gender</td>

Court-ordered, long legal processes involved<sup about="#mwt462" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-2023_ussd_6-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"2023 ussd"}}'><a href="#cite_note-2023_ussd-6" id="mwA-0"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA-4"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA-8">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA_A">]</span></span></a></sup></td></tr>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="19" id="mwBCA" aria-labelledby="Notes"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Notes">Notes</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=LGBTQ_rights_in_Bahrain&amp;action=edit&amp;section=19" title="Edit section: Notes"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt468" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwBCE" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Notelist ","href":"./Template:Notelist"},"params":{"refs":{"wt":"{{efn|name=\"long\"|text=Proposed amendments to the law were described in a January 2022 article in &apos;&apos;Albilad&apos;&apos;&lt;!--And for some reason reproduced in full here! (Please delete or truncate.)-->:{{r|albilad 2022}}\n{{Verse translation|lang1=ar|italicsoff=y|rtl1=y|\nأعادت حادثة توزيع إحدى المدارس الخاصة قصة تروج الشذوذ الجنسي لطلابها - والتي ذكرت وزارة التربية والتعليم أنها تمت عن طريق الخطأ - قضية الشذوذ الجنسي إلى السطح، إذ رفع مجلس النواب في جلسته الاستثنائية الخميس الماضي مقترحًا برغبة بصفة الاستعجال بفتح تحقيق في هذه الحادثة.\nوفي رده على استفسار لـ \"البلاد\" حول معالجة القانون البحريني لفعل الترويج للشذوذ قال المحامي محمود ربيع إن المحرض على الجرم يعتبر مساهما مع الفاعل الأصلي في الجريمة متى أدى التحريض (الترويج) إلى ارتكاب الجريمة.\nوأشار إلى أن المشرع البحريني يُعاقب المساهم الجنائي أو المحرض بذات العقوبة المقررة للفاعل الأصلي، بمعنى إذا كانت عقوبة الفاعل الأصلي في جريمة القتل العمد هو السجن المؤبد أو الإعدام، فإن عقوبة المحرض على هذه الجريمة تكون ذات العقوبة؛ لأنه يعد شريكا في الجريمة من حرض على ارتكابها فوقعت بناء على هذا التحريض.\nولفت إلى أنه على الجانب الآخر، لا يمنع أن يكون التحريض بحد ذاته جريمة، حتى وإن لم ينتج عنه أثر، وهو ما طبقه المشرع البحريني في جرائم معينة، كتلك الماسَّة بأمن الدولة الداخلي التي وردت تفصيلًا في الفصل الثاني من قانون العقوبات البحريني، إضافة إلى ما قرره المشرع في قانون حماية المجتمع من الأعمال الإرهابية المعدل، من عقوبة السجن للمحرض على على ارتكاب جريمة لغرض إرهابي حتى ولو لم يترتب على فعله أثر.\n\nالفجور والدعارة\nوأشار إلى أن قانون العقوبات عاقب في المادة 324 كل من حرض ذكرًا أو أنثى على ارتكاب الفجور والدعارة أو ساعده على ذلك بأي طريقة كانت بالحبس.\nوبين أن مفهوم دلالة نص هذه المادة حسب رأي الفقه والقضاء، أن جريمة الفجور والدعارة لا تتحقق بدورها إلا بمباشرة الفحشاء مع الناس بغير تمييز، وأن يكون ذلك على وجه الاعتياد سواء بالنسبة لبغاء الرجل أو بغاء الأنثى.\nوأضاف أن الأنثى حين ترتكب الفحشاء وتبيح عرضها لكل طالب بلا تمييز فتلك هي الدعارة، تنسب للبغي فلا تصدر إلا منها، ويقابلها الفجور، ينسب للرجل حين يبيح عرضه لغيره من الرجال بغير تمييز فلا يصدر إلا منه.\nورأى أنه ينبغي تعديل النص بإضافة الشذوذ الجنسي الى جانب تجريم التحريض على الفجور والدعارة، لتغطية الفراغ التشريعي، مع ضرورة تعريف الشذوذ الجنسي تعريفا بنص صريح يُجرم الشذوذ الجنسي، ويوضح الفرق بينها وبين جريمتي الفجور والدعارة.\nوذكر أن مهمة القانون تنظيم العلاقات في المجتمع، وإذا ما ظهرت علاقة مؤثمة تتنافى مع قيم المجتمع وأخلاقه، فإن واجب السلطة التشريعية أن تتصدى لهذه الظاهرة الشاذة، وأن ينظم أمرها بتشريع عقابي وصولًا لاستئصالها.\n\nتعديل تشريعي\nويأتي ذلك في وقت تقدم فيه نواب في أكتوبر الماضي باقتراح بقانون بتعديل بعض أحكام قانون العقوبات، في مادته الأولى بتعديل عنوان الفصل الثالث من الباب السابع من القسم الخاص من قانون العقوبات ليكون \"الفجور والدعارة والشذوذ\".\nونص التعديل على إضافة مادتين تنص الأولى على \"يعاقب بالسجن مدة لا تجاوز 5 سنوات والغرامة التي لا تقل عن 3 آلاف دينار ولا تجاوز 5 آلاف دينار أو بإحدى هاتين العقوبتين\":\n\n1- كل من رفع علما أو شعارا أو أية إشارة ترمز إلى فئة الشواذ جنسيا.\n2- كل من روج بأية وسيلة كانت لأفكار ومعتقدات فئة الشواذ جنسيا.\n3- كل من دعا أو نظم أو حضر أي تجمع أو اجتماع لفئة الشواذ جنسيا.\n\nونص التعديل الثاني على أن \"يعاقب الشخص الاعتباري بالغرامة التي لا تقل عن 10 آلاف دينار ولا تزيد على خمسين ألف دينار، إذا ارتكبت إحدى الأفعال المذكورة في المادة السابقة، وكان ذلك نتيجة اتفاق أو علم أعضاء مجلس الإدارة أو أصحاب المؤسسة أو مالكها.\nوفي حالة الحكم بالإدانة تأمر المحكمة بحرمان الشخص الاعتباري من المشاركة في المشروعات العامة أو بإغلاق مقاره التي وقعت فيها الجريمة أو بحله وإغلاق جميع مقاره إغلاقا مؤقتا أو دائما\nوتأمر المحكمة في جميع الأحوال بمصادرة الأموال والأمتعة والأدوات والأوراق مما يكون قد استعمل أو أعد للاستعمال في ارتكاب الجريمة أو تحصل منها، وذلك مع عدم المساس بحقوق غير الحسن النية. \n|The incident of a private school distributing materials allegedly promoting homosexuality to its students — which the Ministry of Education claimed happened by mistake — has brought the issue of homosexuality back to the forefront. Last Thursday, the Parliament in its exceptional session proposed an urgent investigation into the matter.\n\nResponding to an inquiry by \"Al-Bilad\" about how Bahraini law addresses the act of promoting homosexuality, lawyer Mahmoud Rabie stated that inciting a crime is considered contributing to the offense, making the inciter equally culpable with the primary perpetrator if the incitement (promotion) leads to the commission of the crime. He pointed out that Bahraini legislators punish criminal accomplices or inciters with the same penalty prescribed for the primary perpetrator. For example, if the penalty for premeditated murder is life imprisonment or death, the inciter would face the same penalty as they are deemed a partner in the crime if their incitement caused it to occur.\n\nAdditionally, he noted that incitement can be a crime in itself, even if it does not lead to any tangible outcome. Bahraini legislators have applied this principle to specific crimes, such as those endangering state security detailed in the second chapter of the Penal Code, as well as in the amended Law on Protecting Society from Terrorist Acts, which imposes imprisonment on anyone inciting a crime for terrorist purposes, even if their actions have no effect.\n\n&apos;&apos;&apos;Debauchery and Prostitution&apos;&apos;&apos;\nThe Penal Code in Article 324 punishes anyone who incites a man or woman to commit debauchery or prostitution or assists them in any way with imprisonment.\n\nAccording to legal and judicial interpretation, the crime of debauchery and prostitution only occurs through engaging in immoral acts indiscriminately and habitually, whether it involves male or female prostitution.&lt;br/>The lawyer added that a woman committing immoral acts indiscriminately is considered prostitution, attributed solely to her, while debauchery is attributed to a man offering himself to other men indiscriminately.\n\nHe argued for amending the law to include homosexuality alongside debauchery and prostitution to address legislative gaps. The amendment should explicitly define homosexuality and differentiate it from the crimes of debauchery and prostitution. He emphasized that the law&apos;s role is to regulate relationships within society. If a prohibited relationship emerges, conflicting with societal values and morals, it becomes the legislature&apos;s duty to confront the phenomenon and regulate it with punitive legislation to eliminate it.\n\n&apos;&apos;&apos;Legislative Amendment&apos;&apos;&apos;\nIn October, MPs proposed amending the Penal Code&apos;s provisions, including renaming Chapter Three of Part Seven to \"Debauchery, Prostitution, and Homosexuality.\"\nThe amendment introduces two articles, the first stipulating imprisonment for no more than five years and fines between 3,000 and 5,000 Bahraini Dinars (or either penalty) for:\n1- Raising a flag, symbol, or sign representing homosexuality.\n2- Promoting homosexual ideas or beliefs by any means.\n3- Organizing, calling for, or attending homosexual gatherings or meetings.\n\nThe second article imposes a fine between 10,000 and 50,000 Bahraini Dinars on legal entities if such acts are committed under their authority or with the knowledge of their managers, owners, or board members.\n\nIf convicted, the court may ban the entity from public projects, close the premises where the crime occurred, or dissolve the entity entirely, with temporary or permanent closures of all its offices.\n\nThe court will also order the confiscation of funds, equipment, tools, or documents used or intended for use in committing the crime, without affecting the rights of third parties acting in good faith.|attr1=|attr2=}} }}"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1327269900" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles"
